Adam details:
```
m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0; lr=lr_init
while time<deadline:
    p=softmax(x)
    P=rfft(p,L)
    q=irfft(P*P,L)[:2n-1]
    h=n*q
    hmax=float(np.max(h))
    score=2*hmax
    if score < best_local: best_local=score; best_x=x.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0 else no_improve++
    if no_improve > patience and time<deadline:
         # perturb from best_x
         sigma = 0.05 + 0.1*rng.random()
         x = best_x + sigma*lowpass noise? maybe rng.normal
         m[:]=0; v[:]=0; lr=max(lr_final, lr*0.7); no_improve=0; continue
    # T schedule: fraction elapsed in trial
    frac = min(1, iter_count/total_est?) Use time fraction: start_time, trial_end. T = T_init * (T_final/T_init)**frac
    compute weights; if e.sum()==0? no.
    grad...
    t+=1; adam_update(x, grad)
    x -= x.mean()
    # optional if x has large values > 50, scale? To prevent overflow, softmax handles max, but x may drift; centering and Adam okay. Could clip x to [-50,50]? Clipping may hinder. Instead if max(abs(x))>100, rescale x *=100/max.
```

Lowpass noise: 
```
def add_lowpass_noise(p, rng, nfreq, amp):
    n=len(p); t=np.arange(n)/n
    x=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,nfreq+1):
        a=rng.random(); ph=2πrng.random()
        x += (1.0/k**0.7)*amp*np.cos(2π*k*t+ph)
    q = p * np.exp(x)
    return q/q.sum()
```
This keeps support and smooths.

```
def gen_profile_uniform(n): ...
def gen_profile_poly(n, deg, rng): t; coeffs; y=1; for k: y += rng.uniform(-1,1)*t^k; y=np.maximum(y,1e-4); return y/y.sum()
def gen_profile_gauss(n, rng): c=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); w=rng.uniform(0.1,0.4); ...
```
We need no lambdas.

Optimization function details with global best:

Function `update_best(p, score)`:
```
def update_best(p, score):
    global G_BEST_SCORE, G_BEST_A
    if score < G_BEST_SCORE:
       G_BEST_SCORE = score
       G_BEST_A = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(p)
    if score < G_BEST_BY_N.get(n, np.inf):
       G_BEST_BY_N[n] = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64)
```
But returning p copy may be memory. okay.

`fast_score(p)`:
```
def fast_score(p):
    n=len(p); L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    q=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
    # due round tiny negative; q=np.maximum(q,0)
    h=n*q
    return float(2*np.max(h)), h, P, L
```
Inside optimize, avoid function call overhead? okay.

```
else: p0=p0/s
```

Set `L = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()`? Let's implement `next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=2n-1. If n=1, m=1 ->1.

Loop:
```
it=0
start_opt=time.monotonic()
m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
lr=lr_init
t_current=T_init
no_improve=0
best_score=score
best_x=x.copy()
# For T decay, use it relative to T裁员
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    it +=1
    # T schedule: T = T_init * (T_final/T_init)**min(1, it/30000)
    frac = min(1.0, it / 30000.0)
    T = T_init * (T_final / T_init) ** frac
    if it > 5000: T = max(T, T_final)
    # compute P/q/h; maybe if p all zero? no
    p = np.exp(x - x.max()); p /= p.sum()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)
    q = q[:2*n-1]
    # q can be slightly negative; for h max use max(0)
    if q.size:
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    h = n * q
    hmax = float(h.max())
    score = 2.0 * hmax
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
         best_score=score; best_x=x.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0; update_best(p,score)
    else: no_improve +=1
    if no_improve > patience:
         # restart
         scale = 0.05 + 0.2 * rng.random()
         # generate lowpass noise? maybe simply standard_normal
         noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
         # low-pass optional: if n>512, noise=np.convolve(noise, np.ones(3)/3, 'same')? costly. Use random smooth:
         noise = add_lowpass_noise_to_x(n, rng, max(1,n//64), 1.0)
         x = best_x + scale * noise
         x -= x.mean()
         m[:]=0; v[:]=0; lr = max(lr_final, lr*0.6); no_improve=0; patience=max(200, patience//2)
         continue
    # weights
    # h can be zero? if score 0 impossible.
    # to avoid underflow, compute log weights
    # If T very small and hmax very small? T>0.
    diff = (h - hmax) / T
    diff = np.where(diff < -700, -700, diff)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    e_sum = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(e_sum) or e_sum <=0:
         e = np.zeros_like(h); idx=int(np.argmax(h)); e[idx]=1.0; e_sum=1.0
    w = e / e_sum
    Qh = float(np.dot(w, h))
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    R = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(P), L)[:n]
    R = np.maximum(R, 0.0)  # should be nonnegative
    g = n * R - Qh
    # center g? Since sum p*g=0 but sum not exactly; we can subtract mean? In x nullspace, only mean matters; but gradient mean should be ~0. Centering g reduces drift: g -= g.mean()? Does that alter descent? Since softmax invariant under constant x shift, constant component of gradient irrelevant; subtracting mean okay.
    g -= g.mean()
    grad = 2.0 * p * g
    # Clip grad to avoid huge? 
    # Adam
    t_adam += 1
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
    v = b2*v + (1-b2)*grad*grad
    mh = m / (1 - b1**t_adam)
    vh = v / (1 - b2**t_adam)
    x -= lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    x -= x.mean()
    max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
    if max_abs > 80:
         x *= 80.0/max_abs
    # lr schedule
    lr = lr_init * max(1.0, lr_final/lr_init) ** frac  # decrease with same fraction
```

Adding noise function top-level:
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq, amp):
    nfreq = min(nfreq, n//2)
    t = np.arange(n)/n
    out = np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1, nfreq+1):
        coeff = amp / (k**0.7)
        ph = 2*np.pi*rng.random()
        out += coeff * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t + ph)
    return out
```
But per restart looping up to n/64 (64 for 4096) cost okay. For many restarts maybe. Could use a single white noise and FFT low-pass? Simpler.

Initial profiles random smooth: Use x generation with lowpass_noise then softmax. We can generate p directly:
```
def random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=12, amp=1.0):
    x = lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq, amp)
    x = x - x.mean()
    # add small white noise
    x = 0.9*x + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)
    p = np.exp(x - x.max()); p/=p.sum(); return p
```
Need clamp positive.

Function `internal_score(p)` top-level:
```
def internal_score(p):
    n=len(p); L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); q=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; q=np.maximum(q,0); return 2.0*float(np.max(n*q))
```
Will recompute FFT; okay for parent/candidates. In optimize, we compute simultaneously.

Let's implement profile generation.

`make_initial_candidate(n, idx, rng, parent)`:
```
def make_initial_candidate(n, idx, rng, parent):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    style = idx % 10
    if style == 0:
        p = np.ones(n)
    elif style == 1:
        a = rng.uniform(0.3,2.0)
        p = 1.0 + a * t
        p2 = 1.0 + rng.uniform(0.3,2.0) * (1.0 - t)
        p = np.maximum(p, p2) ? maybe choose one.
        p = np.clip(p, 1e-5, None)
    elif style == 2:
        c = rng.uniform(0.25,0.75); w=rng.uniform(0.1,0.35)
        p = np.exp(-0.5*(((t-c)/w)**2))
    elif style == 3:
        beta = rng.choice([-1,1]) * rng.uniform(0.5,3.0)
        p = np.exp(beta*t)
    elif style == 4:
        f = rng.randint(1,6); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); a=rng.uniform(0.2,0.9)
        p = 1.0 + a*np.cos(2π*f*t+ph)
    elif style == 5:
        # polynomial random
        deg = rng.randint(2,6)
        p = np.ones(n)
        for d in range(1,deg+1):
            p += rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*(t**d)
        p = np.maximum(p,1e-5)
    elif style == 6:
        p = random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=rng.randint(4,16), amp=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5))
    elif style == 7:
        # two gaussians
        c1=...; p = exp + amp*exp
    elif style == 8:
        # smooth white with high amp
        p = random_smooth_p(...)
    elif style == 9:
        # parent perturb if available
        if parent is not None:
            q = resample_mass(parent, n)
            return perturb_p(q,rng,rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
        else: uniform
    p = np.asarray(p,float); p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=...; p = p/s
    # optionally small white noise to avoid exact symmetries
    p = 1.0 + rng.standard_normal(n)*0.01? But can distort; maybe clip.
    return p
```
Symmetric profiles may have local minima; adding small random asymmetry helpful. We can add small smooth noise to all profiles: `p = perturb_p(p, rng, 0.02)` maybe. But perturb uses log multiply by exp(noise). Good.

`perturb_p(p, rng, amp)`:
```
x = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); x -= x.mean()
x += amp * lowpass_noise(len(x), rng, max(1, len(x)//32), 1.0)
x += 0.01 * amp * rng.standard_normal(len(x))? optional
q = np.exp(x - x.max()); return q/q.sum()
```

```
def resample_mass(p_src, n_dst):
    p=np.asarray(p_src,float).ravel()
    m=len(p)
    if m==n_dst: return p/p.sum()
    p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/n
    p=p/s
    x_src = np.concatenate([np.linspace(0,1,m+1)??])
```

Detailed code for optimize:
```
def optimize_from_p(p0, deadline, rng, tag, T_init=0.10, T_final=0.003, lr_init=0.25, lr_final=0.02, patience=2000, max_attempts=10):
    global G_BEST_SCORE, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_BY_N
    n = len(p0)
    if n < 2: return
    # normalize and ensure positive
    p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p0 = np.maximum(p0, 0.0)
    s = p0.sum()
    if s <= 0.0:
        p0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        p0 = p0 / s
    # add tiny floor before log
    p0 = np.maximum(p0, 1e-300)
    x = np.log(p0)
    x -= x.max()
    L = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    # initial score
    p = normalize? from x
    p = np.exp(x - x.max()); p /= p.sum()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    h = n*q
    best_score = 2.0*float(np.max(h))
    if best_score < G_BEST_SCORE: update_best(p,best_score)
    x_best = x.copy()
    p_best = p.copy()
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n)
    t_adam = 0
    adj = np.zeros(n) # maybe lambda? no
    start = time.monotonic()
    trial_target_time = max(1.0, deadline - start)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    restarts = 0
    patience_cur = patience
    lr = lr_init
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / trial_target_time)
        # if trial_target_time changes? no.
        T = T_init * (T_final / T_init) ** frac
        lr = lr_init * (lr_final / lr_init) ** frac
        # softmax p
        x_max = float(np.max(x))
        p = np.exp(x - x_max)
        p_sum = p.sum()
        if p_sum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(p_sum):
             p = np.ones(n)/n; x = np.zeros(n)
        else:
             p = p / p_sum
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        qq = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2*n-1]
        # clip small imaginary? irfft real
        # negative due round
        if qq.min() < 0:
             qq = np.maximum(qq, 0.0)
        h = n * qq
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        score = 2.0 * hmax
        if np.isfinite(score):
             if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                  best_score = score
                  x_best = x.copy()
                  p_best = p.copy()
                  no_improve = 0
                  update_best(p_best, best_score)
             else:
                  no_improve += 1
        else:
             # invalid, restart
             no_improve = patience_cur
        if no_improve >= patience_cur:
             restarts += 1
             if restarts > 20:
                  break
             # perturb best
             amp = 0.05 + 0.15 * rng.random()
             noise = lowpass_noise(n, rng, max(1, n//32), 1.0)
             noise += 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n)
             x = x_best + amp * noise
             x -= x.mean()
             m[:] = 0.0; v[:] = 0.0; t_adam=0
             lr = max(lr_final, lr * 0.7)
             no_improve = 0
             patience_cur = max(300, patience_cur // 2)
             continue
        # weights
        # If hmax very small? set T relative
        if hmax > 1e-12:
             diff = (h - hmax) / T
        else:
             diff = np.zeros_like(h)
        # clip diff
        # Use np.clip for speed
        np.clip(diff, -700.0, 0.0, out=diff)  # diff <=0
        e = np.exp(diff)
        e_sum = e.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(e_sum) or e_sum <= 0.0:
             e = np.zeros_like(h)
             e[int(np.argmax(h))] = 1.0
             e_sum = 1.0
        w = e / e_sum
        Qh = float(np.dot(w, h))
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        R = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(P), L)[:n]
        # due numerical, R should >=0; clip
        np.maximum(R, 0.0, out=R)
        g = n * R - Qh
        # Remove mean component (nullspace)
        g -= float(np.mean(g))
        grad = 2.0 * p * g
        # optionally clip grad to avoid huge values where p small? Adam handles.
        t_adam += 1
        b1 = 0.9; b2 = 0.999; eps = 1e-8
        b1_t = b1 ** t_adam
        b2_t = b2 ** t_adam
        m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
        v = b2*v + (1-b2)*grad*grad
        mh = m / (1 - b1_t)
        vh = v / (1 - b2_t)
        x -= lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)
        # clean
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
             x = x_best.copy(); continue
        x -= float(np.mean(x))
        max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
        if max_abs > 80.0:
             x *= 80.0 / max_abs
    # after loop, maybe update best with p_best
    if best_score < G_BEST_SCORE:
         G_BEST_SCORE=best_score; G_BEST_A=np.array(p_best)
    if best_score < G_BEST_BY_N.get(n, np.inf):
         G_BEST_BY_N[n]=np.array(p_best)
```

Let's write `make_initial_candidate` robust:
```
def make_initial_candidate(n, idx, rng, parent):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    style = idx % 12
    if style == 0:
        p = np.ones(n)
    elif style == 1:
        a = rng.uniform(0.2, 2.5)
        p = 1.0 + a * t
    elif style == 2:
        a = rng.uniform(0.2, 2.5)
        p = 1.0 + a * (1.0 - t)
    elif style == 3:
        c = rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); w = rng.uniform(0.08,0.35)
        p = np.exp(-0.5*((t-c)/w)**2)
    elif style == 4:
        c1 = rng.uniform(0.1,0.4); c2=rng.uniform(0.6,0.9); w=...
        p = 0.3*np.exp(...) + np.exp(...)
    elif style == 5:
        beta = rng.choice((-1,1)) * rng.uniform(0.5,4.0)
        p = np.exp(beta*t)
    elif style == 6:
        f = rng.randint(1,8); ph=...; a=rng.uniform(0.2,0.9)
        p = 1.0 + a*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t+ph)
    elif style == 7:
        # odd asymmetric polynomial
        p = 1.0 + rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*t + rng.uniform(0,1)*t*t + rng.uniform(-0.2,0.2)*t*t*t
    elif style == 8:
        p = random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=rng.randint(3,12), amp=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5))
    elif style == 9:
        p = random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=rng.randint(12,32), amp=rng.uniform(0.8,2.0))
    elif style == 10:
        # sine with linear trend
        f = rng.randint(1,5); ph=...; a=rng.uniform(0.3,1.0); b=rng.uniform(-1,1)
        p = 1.0 + b*t + a*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t+ph)
    else:
        # piecewise blocks
        nb = rng.randint(2,8)
        blocks = rng.uniform(0.3,1.5,nb)
        p = np.repeat(blocks, n//nb)
        if len(p)<n: p=np.concatenate([p, blocks[:n-len(p)]])
        p = np.clip(p,1e-4,None)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
    # small smooth perturbation to break symmetry unless it is random smooth already? Do all.
    amp = rng.uniform(0.02, 0.10)
    p = perturb_p(p, rng, amp)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0: p = np.ones(n)/n
    else: p = p/s
    return p
```
For random smooth, applying perturb again okay.

`random_smooth_p` uses lowpass x and softmax. It may produce p with very small values; perturb handles.

`perturb_p` should not make p zeros; it uses exp. Ensure input p positive; if zeros, use floor. Implementation:
```
def perturb_p(p, rng, amp):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel(); n=len(p)
    p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    p=p/s
    x=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); x-=x.mean()
    nfreq=min(max(1,n//32), n//2)
    noise=lowpass_noise(n,rng,nfreq,1.0)
    x += amp*noise
    # small white
    x += 0.02*amp*rng.standard_normal(n)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    q=np.exp(x-x.max())
    q=q/q.sum()
    return q
```
If amp large and noise, p may become skewed; okay.

`lowpass_noise` if n small: nfreq min(n//2). If n=2, nfreq=1. Good.

```
def random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=16, amp=1.0):
```

```
    restarts += 1
    if restarts > 30: break

    noise=lowpass_noise(n,rng,max(1,n//64),1.0)

    x-=x.mean()
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

G_BEST_SCORE = np.inf
...

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def normalize_prob(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / p.size
    return p / s

def internal_score(p):
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0: return np.inf
    L = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2*n-1]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    if q.size == 0: return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(np.max(n * q))
```
`n*q` creates array; okay.

`lowpass_noise`:
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq, amp):
    if n <=0: return np.array([])
    nfreq = int(max(1, min(nfreq, n//2)))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, nfreq+1):
        ph = 2.0*np.pi*rng.random()
        out += (amp / (float(k) ** 0.7)) * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*t + ph)
    return out
```
If n=1, n//2=0, max(1,0)=1 but min(1,0)=0? Need handle: `max_freq = max(1, n//2)`? For n=1, max_freq=1 but nfreq min? Let's do:
```
limit = max(1, n // 2)
nfreq = int(min(max(1, nfreq), limit))
```
For n=1 limit1. Good.

`random_smooth_p`:
```
def random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=16, amp=1.0):
    x = lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq, amp)
    x = 0.85*x + 0.15*(amp*rng.standard_normal(n))
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    p = np.exp(x - np.max(x))
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return ones/n
    return p/s
```

`perturb_p` as above.

`resample_mass`:
```
def resample_mass(p_src, n_dst):
    p = normalize_prob(p_src)
    m = p.size
    if m == 0 or n_dst == 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if m == n_dst: return p.copy()
    x_src = np.arange(m+1, dtype=float) / float(m)
    y_src = np.cumsum(p); y_src = np.concatenate(([0.0], y_src))
    # ensure last 1
    y_src[-1]=1.0
    x_dst = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_dst+1)
    c = np.interp(x_dst, x_src, y_src)
    p_dst = np.diff(c)
    p_dst = np.clip(p_dst,0.0,None)
    s=p_dst.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n_dst)/n_dst
    return p_dst/s
```
For m=1, x_src [0,1], y [0,1], interp okay.

`make_initial_candidate` as above. Need if `parent` not used? We pass but not use except maybe style? We don't use; okay. Could use style 11 to perturb parent? But parent available. We'll keep parameter but unused? To avoid lint, use in one style:
```
else: # style 11
    if parent is not None:
        p = resample_mass(parent, n)
    else:
        p = np.ones(n)/n
    p = perturb_p(p, rng, rng.uniform(0.1,0.4))
    return normalize_prob(p)
```


`optimize_from_p` detailed code. Need to ensure variables defined if initial score invalid. Let's write:
```
def optimize_from_p(p0, deadline, rng, tag, T_init=0.10, T_final=0.004, lr_init=0.25, lr_final=0.02, patience_iters=10000):
    global ...
    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= now: return
    p0 = normalize_prob(p0)
    n = p0.size
    if n < 2: return
    x = np.log(np.maximum(p0, 1e-300))
    x -= float(np.max(x))
    L = next_pow2(2*n -1)
    p = np.exp(x - np.max(x)); p /= p.sum()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; q=np.maximum(q,0)
    score = 2.0*float(np.max(n*q))
    if not np.isfinite(score): score=np.inf
    best_score = score
    x_best = x.copy(); p_best = p.copy()
    update_best(p_best, best_score)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0; b1_t=1.0; b2_t=1.0
    start=now; span=max(1.0, deadline-start)
    lr_scale=1.0; restarts=0; last_best=start; it=0; grad_zero=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic(); it+=1
       frac=min(1.0, (now-start)/span)
       T=T_init * (T_final/T_init)**frac
       lr=lr_init * (lr_final/lr_init)**frac * lr_scale
       x_max=float(np.max(x)); p=np.exp(x-x_max); ps=p.sum()
       if not finite ps or ps<=0: p=ones/n; x=zeros
       else: p=p/ps
       P=...
       q=...; h=n*q; hmax=float(np.max(h)); score=2*hmax
       if finite and score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; x_best=x.copy(); p_best=p.copy(); last_best=now; update_best(p_best,best_score)
       if now - last_best > max(1.0, 0.25*span):
          restarts+=1
          if restarts>40: break
          amp=0.05+0.25*rng.random()
          noise=lowpass_noise(n,rng,max(1,n//64),1.0)
          noise += 0.25*rng.standard_normal(n)
          x=x_best + amp*noise; x-=x.mean()
          m[:]=0; v[:]=0; t_adam=0; b1_t=1; b2_t=1; lr_scale=max(0.25, lr_scale*0.7); last_best=now; continue
       # weights
       if hmax > 1e-300:
           diff=(h-hmax)/T
       else: diff=np.zeros_like(h)
       diff=np.nan_to_num(diff, nan=-700.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-700.0)
       np.minimum(diff, 0.0, out=diff)
       e=np.exp(diff); e_sum=float(e.sum())
       if not finite or e_sum<=0:
           e=np.zeros_like(h); e[int(np.argmax(h))]=1.0; e_sum=1.0
       w=e/e_sum
       active=np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-12)
       if active.size <= 80:
           Qh=float(np.dot(w[active], h[active])) if active.size else 0.0
           R=np.zeros(n)
           for ii in range(active.size):
              k=int(active[ii]); wi=float(w[k])
              if wi <=0: continue
              j_start = k - (n - 1)
              if j_start < 0: j_start=0
              j_end = k
              if j_end >= n: j_end = n-1
              if j_start <= j_end:
                  start_idx = k - j_end
                  stop_idx = k - j_start + 1
                  R[j_start:j_end+1] += wi * p[start_idx:stop_idx][::-1]
       else:
           Qh=float(np.dot(w,h))
           W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
           R=np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(P), L)[:n]
       np.maximum(R,0.0,out=R)
       g = n*R - Qh
       grad = 2.0*p*g
       grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
       maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
       if maxg < 1e-14:
           grad_zero += 1
           if grad_zero > 100:
               # force a tiny noise to escape exact saddle
               x += 0.01*rng.standard_normal(n); x-=x.mean(); grad_zero=0
           else:
               grad = 0.01*lowpass_noise(n,rng,max(1,n//128),1.0)
               grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
       else: grad_zero=0
       t_adam +=1
       b1=0.9; b2=0.999
       b1_t *= b1; b2_t *= b2
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
       v = b2*v + (1-b2)*grad*grad
       mh = m/(1-b1_t); vh=v/(1-b2_t)
       x -= lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
       x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
       x -= float(np.mean(x))
       max_abs=float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
       if max_abs > 80.0: x *= 80.0/max_abs
    # end
    if np.isfinite(best_score):
       update_best(p_best,best_score)
       if best_score < G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N.get(n, np.inf):
           G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N[n]=best_score
           G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[n]=np.array(p_best, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    print("opt ended tag=%s n=%d best=%.6f" % (tag,n,best_score))
```
Concern: In grad_zero branch, if maxg small, setting grad to noise may cause progress but maybe not downhill. It's okay for escape. Alternatively just continue without update? But then no change. We'll add noise grad.

In restart, we don't update T; fraction based on time maybe near end. Fine.

Potential issue: `active` size can be 0 if threshold all below; then Qh=0,R=0,grad=2p(0) after subtract mean zero. But e has at least one > threshold? max weight could be 1 if all under? Actually normalize, max≥1/(2n-1). For n=4096, 1e-4 >1e-12. So active≥1.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global ...
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    total = max(10.0, budget - 20.0)
    deadline = start + total
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    G_BEST_SCORE=np.inf; G_BEST_A=None; G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N={}; G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N={}
    parent=None
    try: ...
    if parent is not None:
       sc=internal_score(parent)
       if finite: update_best(parent, sc)
    phases = [...]
    cum=0.0
    for (n, frac, max_trial, T_init, T_final, lr_init, lr_final, n_trials) in phases:
       cum += frac
       phase_end = start + cum*total
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= phase_end: continue
       span = phase_end - now
       if span < 3.0: continue
       trial_time = min(float(max_trial), max(1.0, span / float(max(1,n_trials))))
       inits=[]; tags=[]
       if n in G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N:
          inits.append(np.array(G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[n], copy=True)); tags.append('best'%n)
       if G_BEST_A is not None:
          pr = resample_mass(G_BEST_A, n)
          inits.append(pr); tags.append('gres')
       if parent is not None:
          inits.append(resample_mass(parent,n)); tags.append('parent')
       # perturb best
       if G_BEST_A is not None and len(inits) < n_trials:
          inits.append(perturb_p(resample_mass(G_BEST_A,n), rng, rng.uniform(0.05,0.25))); tags.append('gperturb')
       # fill random
       style_idx = 0
       while len(inits) < n_trials:

          inits.append(p); tags.append('style%d'%style_idx)
          style_idx += 1
       # allocate trial_time maybe adapt if too many inits: if len(inits)>0, ensure sum? no.
       idx=0
       for idx,p in enumerate(inits):
           now=time.monotonic()

           opt_end = min(phase_end, now + trial_time)
           if opt_end - now < 0.5: break
           optimize_from_p(p, opt_end, rng, tags[idx], T_init, T_final, lr_init, lr_final)
       # after inits, if time remains, optimize current best at n
       now=time.monotonic()

           optimize_from_p(G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[n], phase_end, rng, 'phasebest%d'%n, T_init*0.7, T_final*0.7, lr_init*0.7, lr_final*0.7)

    # final
    now=time.monotonic()
    final_end = deadline - 2.0
    if G_BEST_A is None:
       G_BEST_A = np.ones(2048)/2048.0
       update_best(G_BEST_A, internal_score(G_BEST_A))
    pfinal = np.array(G_BEST_A, copy=True)
    # cap/resample
    if pfinal.size > 8192:
        pfinal = resample_mass(pfinal, 4096)
        sc=internal_score(pfinal)
        if sc < G_BEST_SCORE: update_best(pfinal, sc)
        pfinal = resample_mass(G_BEST_A if G_BEST_A is not None else pfinal, 4096)
    elif pfinal.size < 4096 and (final_end - time.monotonic()) > 120:
        cand = resample_mass(pfinal, 4096)
        sc=internal_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= G_BEST_SCORE * 1.005:
            pfinal = cand
            if sc < G_BEST_SCORE: update_best(cand, sc)
    # final optimize
    T_init_final = 0.05 if pfinal.size <= 2048 else 0.035
    T_final_final = 0.001 if pfinal.size <= 2048 else 0.0007
    lr_init_final = 0.10 if pfinal.size <=2048 else 0.08
    lr_final_final = 0.01
    if final_end > time.monotonic() + 2:
       optimize_from_p(pfinal, final_end, rng, 'final', T_init_final, T_final_final, lr_init_final, lr_final_final)
    # choose best
    if G_BEST_A is None: fallback uniform 2048
    else pout = normalize_prob(G_BEST_A)
    if pout.size > 8192: pout=resample_mass(pout,4096)
    pout = np.clip(pout,0,1000)
    s=pout.sum(); if s<0.01: pout=np.ones(2048)/2048 else: pout=pout/s
    out=[float(x) for x in pout]
    try: verified=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception: verified=internal_score(out)
    print(...)
    return out
```

```python
"""
Multi-scale softmax-Adam optimizer for
    F(a) = 2 * n * max_k conv(a, a)[k] / (sum a)^2 .

We optimize probability vectors p = a / sum(a) using
    x -> p = softmax(x)
and a log-sum-exp smoothing of max_k (n * conv(p,p)[k]).

The FFT gradient is computed in closed form.  Hybrids are used:
when the log-sum-exp weights are sparse, the weighted correlation is
computed by direct slicing; otherwise an FFT correlation is used.

The search is multi-scale:
  - low-resolution random smooth profiles for global exploration,
  - resampling of the best incumbents to larger lengths,
  - final polishing at a larger length.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global best trackers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

G_BEST_SCORE = np.inf
G_BEST_A = None
G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N = {}
G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N = {}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Utilities
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_prob(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def internal_score(p):
    """Fast FFT-based evaluation of the objective, assuming normalization."""
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * float(np.max(n * q))


def update_best(p, score):
    global G_BEST_SCORE, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N, G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return
    if score < G_BEST_SCORE:
        G_BEST_SCORE = float(score)
        G_BEST_A = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = int(p.size)
    if score < G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N.get(n, np.inf):
        G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N[n] = float(score)
        G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[n] = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Resampling and random smooth initializations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def resample_mass(p_src, n_dst):
    """Redistribute probability mass from p_src to n_dst bins without drift."""
    p = normalize_prob(p_src)
    m = p.size
    if m == 0 or n_dst <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n_dst:
        return p.copy()

    x_src = np.arange(m + 1, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    y_src = np.cumsum(p)
    y_src = np.empty_like(y_src)
    y_src[0] = 0.0
    y_src[1:] = np.cumsum(p)
    y_src[-1] = 1.0

    x_dst = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_dst + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.interp(x_dst, x_src, y_src)
    p_dst = np.diff(c)
    p_dst = np.clip(p_dst, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p_dst.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n_dst, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_dst)
    return p_dst / s


def lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq, amp):
    """Smooth random field made of a few cosine modes."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    lim = max(1, n // 2)
    nfreq = int(min(max(1, nfreq), lim))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, nfreq + 1):
        ph = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        out += (amp / (float(k) ** 0.7)) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
    return out


def random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=16, amp=1.0):
    x = lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq, amp)
    x = 0.85 * x + 0.15 * (amp * rng.standard_normal(n))
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    p = np.exp(x - float(np.max(x)))
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return p / s


def perturb_p(p, rng, amp):
    """Multiplicative smooth perturbation on the simplex."""
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    n = p.size
    x = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    x -= float(np.mean(x))
    x += amp * lowpass_noise(n, rng, max(1, n // 64), 1.0)
    x += 0.02 * amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    q = np.exp(x - float(np.max(x)))
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return q / s


def make_initial_candidate(n, idx, rng, parent):
    """A variety of asymmetric smooth/structured initial probability vectors."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    style = idx % 12

    if style == 0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif style == 1:
        p = 1.0 + rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5) * t
    elif style == 2:
        p = 1.0 + rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5) * (1.0 - t)
    elif style == 3:
        c = rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8)
        w = rng.uniform(0.08, 0.35)
        p = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c) / w) ** 2)
    elif style == 4:
        c1 = rng.uniform(0.1, 0.4)
        c2 = rng.uniform(0.6, 0.9)
        w1 = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.2)
        w2 = rng.uniform(0.1, 0.35)
        p = 0.45 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c1) / w1) ** 2) + np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c2) / w2) ** 2)
    elif style == 5:
        beta = rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0]) * rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0)
        p = np.exp(beta * t)
    elif style == 6:
        f = rng.randint(1, 8)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a = rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9)
        p = 1.0 + a * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
    elif style == 7:
        p = (
            1.0
            + rng.uniform(-0.4, 0.4) * t
            + rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0) * t * t
            + rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.25) * t ** 3
        )
    elif style == 8:
        p = random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=rng.randint(3, 12), amp=rng.uniform(0.5, 1.5))
    elif style == 9:
        p = random_smooth_p(n, rng, nfreq=rng.randint(12, 32), amp=rng.uniform(1.0, 2.5))
    elif style == 10:
        f = rng.randint(1, 5)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0)
        b = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
        p = 1.0 + b * t + a * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
    else:
        if parent is not None:
            p = resample_mass(parent, n)
        else:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = perturb_p(p, rng, rng.uniform(0.08, 0.35))
        p = normalize_prob(p)
        return p

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
    p = perturb_p(p, rng, rng.uniform(0.01, 0.08))
    return normalize_prob(p)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Core optimizer on softmax variables
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def optimize_from_p(p0, deadline, rng, tag,
                    T_init=0.10, T_final=0.004,
                    lr_init=0.25, lr_final=0.02):
    """
    Optimize p on the simplex using x = log p (softmax coordinate).
    Objective gradient uses a log-sum-exp surrogate of max(n*conv(p,p)).
    """
    global G_BEST_SCORE, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N, G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N

    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= now:
        return

    p0 = normalize_prob(p0)
    n = p0.size
    if n < 2:
        return

    x = np.log(np.maximum(p0, 1e-300))
    x -= float(np.max(x))

    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    # Initial evaluation.
    p = np.exp(x - float(np.max(x)))
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / ps

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    score = 2.0 * float(np.max(n * q))
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = np.inf

    best_score = score
    x_best = x.copy()
    p_best = p.copy()
    update_best(p_best, best_score)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_adam = 0
    b1_t = 1.0
    b2_t = 1.0
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    start = time.monotonic()
    span = max(1.0, deadline - start)
    lr_scale = 1.0
    restarts = 0
    last_best = start
    grad_zero_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        frac = min(1.0, (now - start) / span)

        T = T_init * (T_final / T_init) ** frac
        lr = lr_init * (lr_final / lr_init) ** frac * lr_scale

        # ----- softmax --------------------------------------------
        x_max = float(np.max(x))
        p = np.exp(x - x_max)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = p / ps

        # ----- convolution and current exact score ----------------
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        h = n * q
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        score = 2.0 * hmax

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(score)
            x_best = x.copy()
            p_best = p.copy()
            last_best = now
            update_best(p_best, best_score)

        # ----- stall-based restart --------------------------------
        stall_limit = max(1.0, 0.25 * span)
        if (now - last_best) > stall_limit:
            restarts += 1
            if restarts > 50:
                break
            amp = 0.05 + 0.25 * rng.random()
            noise = lowpass_noise(n, rng, max(1, n // 64), 1.0)
            noise += 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            x = x_best + amp * noise
            x -= float(np.mean(x))
            m[:] = 0.0
            v[:] = 0.0
            t_adam = 0
            b1_t = 1.0
            b2_t = 1.0
            lr_scale = max(0.25, lr_scale * 0.7)
            last_best = now
            continue

        # ----- log-sum-exp weights --------------------------------
        if hmax > 1e-300:
            diff = (h - hmax) / T
        else:
            diff = np.zeros_like(h)
        diff = np.nan_to_num(diff, nan=-700.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-700.0)
        np.minimum(diff, 0.0, out=diff)

        e = np.exp(diff)
        e_sum = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(e_sum) or e_sum <= 0.0:
            e = np.zeros_like(h)
            e[int(np.argmax(h))] = 1.0
            e_sum = 1.0
        w = e / e_sum

        # ----- weighted gradient components -----------------------
        # R_j = sum_k w_k p_{k-j}
        active = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-12)
        if active.size <= 80:
            if active.size > 0:
                Qh = float(np.dot(w[active], h[active]))
            else:
                Qh = 0.0
            R = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for ii in range(active.size):
                k = int(active[ii])
                wi = float(w[k])
                if wi <= 0.0:
                    continue
                j_start = k - (n - 1)
                if j_start < 0:
                    j_start = 0
                j_end = k
                if j_end >= n:
                    j_end = n - 1
                if j_start <= j_end:
                    start_idx = k - j_end
                    stop_idx = k - j_start + 1
                    R[j_start:j_end + 1] += wi * p[start_idx:stop_idx][::-1]
        else:
            Qh = float(np.dot(w, h))
            W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
            R = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(P), L)[:n]

        np.maximum(R, 0.0, out=R)

        # grad wrt x_j for log-sum-exp objective
        g = n * R - Qh
        grad = 2.0 * p * g
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        # Remove the softmax null-space component.
        grad -= float(np.mean(grad))

        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if maxg < 1e-14:
            grad_zero_count += 1
            if grad_zero_count > 100:
                # Force a tiny escape from an exact first-order flat point.
                x += 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                x -= float(np.mean(x))
                grad_zero_count = 0
            else:
                grad = 0.02 * lowpass_noise(n, rng, max(1, n // 128), 1.0)
                grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
        else:
            grad_zero_count = 0

        # ----- Adam step ------------------------------------------
        t_adam += 1
        b1_t *= b1
        b2_t *= b2

        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * grad * grad
        mh = m / (1.0 - b1_t)
        vh = v / (1.0 - b2_t)

        x -= lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
        x -= float(np.mean(x))

        max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
        if max_abs > 80.0:
            x *= 80.0 / max_abs

    if np.isfinite(best_score):
        update_best(p_best, best_score)
        if best_score < G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N.get(n, np.inf):
            G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N[n] = float(best_score)
            G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[n] = np.array(p_best, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)

    print("opt ended tag=%s n=%d best=%.6f" % (tag, n, best_score))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main search driver
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global G_BEST_SCORE, G_BEST_A, G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N, G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N

    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    total = max(10.0, budget - 20.0)
    deadline = start + total
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Reset trackers for this call.
    G_BEST_SCORE = np.inf
    G_BEST_A = None
    G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N = {}
    G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N = {}

    # Optional parent construction from the global variable.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent_arr.size > 0:
            parent_arr = np.clip(parent_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(parent_arr.sum())
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                parent = parent_arr / s
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        sc = internal_score(parent)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            update_best(parent, sc)

    # Multi-scale phases: (n, fraction_of_budget, max_trial_seconds,
    #                       T_init, T_final, lr_init, lr_final, n_trials)
    phases = [
        (512,  0.12, 15.0, 0.12, 0.008, 0.30, 0.05, 12),
        (1024, 0.16, 20.0, 0.10, 0.005, 0.28, 0.04, 12),
        (2048, 0.26, 35.0, 0.09, 0.004, 0.25, 0.035, 12),
        (4096, 0.36, 60.0, 0.08, 0.003, 0.22, 0.03, 12),
    ]

    cum_frac = 0.0
    for (n, frac, max_trial, T_init, T_final, lr_init, lr_final, n_trials) in phases:
        cum_frac += frac
        phase_end = start + cum_frac * total
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= phase_end:
            continue
        span = phase_end - now
        if span < 3.0:
            continue

        trial_time = min(float(max_trial), max(1.0, span / float(max(1, n_trials))))

        inits = []
        tags = []

        if n in G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N:
            inits.append(np.array(G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[n], dtype=np.float64, copy=True))
            tags.append("best_n%d" % n)

        if G_BEST_A is not None:
            inits.append(resample_mass(G_BEST_A, n))
            tags.append("glob_res_%d" % n)

        if parent is not None:
            inits.append(resample_mass(parent, n))
            tags.append("parent_%d" % n)

        if G_BEST_A is not None and len(inits) < n_trials:
            inits.append(perturb_p(resample_mass(G_BEST_A, n), rng, rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25)))
            tags.append("glob_pert_%d" % n)

        style_idx = 0
        while len(inits) < n_trials:
            p = make_initial_candidate(n, style_idx, rng, parent)
            inits.append(p)
            tags.append("style%d_%d" % (style_idx, n))
            style_idx += 1

        for idx in range(len(inits)):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= phase_end:
                break
            opt_end = min(phase_end, now + trial_time)
            if opt_end - now < 0.5:
                break
            optimize_from_p(inits[idx], opt_end, rng, tags[idx],
                            T_init, T_final, lr_init, lr_final)

        # If this phase ended early, spend leftover time on the best at this n.
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < phase_end - 3.0 and n in G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N:
            optimize_from_p(G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[n], phase_end, rng,
                            "phase_best_%d" % n,
                            max(0.001, T_init * 0.7),
                            max(0.0005, T_final * 0.7),
                            max(0.001, lr_init * 0.7),
                            max(0.001, lr_final * 0.7))

        print("phase done n=%d current_best=%.6f elapsed=%.1fs" %
              (n, G_BEST_SCORE, time.monotonic() - start))

    # Final polishing.
    now = time.monotonic()
    final_end = deadline - 2.0

    if G_BEST_A is None:
        fallback = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        G_BEST_A = fallback
        G_BEST_SCORE = internal_score(fallback)
        G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N[2048] = G_BEST_SCORE
        G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[2048] = fallback.copy()

    # Keep final length within a safe range for the evaluator.
    if G_BEST_A is not None and G_BEST_A.size > 8192:
        G_BEST_A = resample_mass(G_BEST_A, 4096)
        sc = internal_score(G_BEST_A)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            G_BEST_SCORE = float(sc)
            G_BEST_A = normalize_prob(G_BEST_A)
            G_BEST_SCORE_BY_N[4096] = float(G_BEST_SCORE)
            G_BEST_ARRAY_BY_N[4096] = np.array(G_BEST_A, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)

    p_final = normalize_prob(G_BEST_A)

    # If the best incumbent is still small and enough time remains, try a
    # larger instance of the same shape.
    remaining = final_end - time.monotonic()
    if p_final.size < 4096 and remaining > 150.0:
        cand = resample_mass(p_final, 4096)
        sc = internal_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= G_BEST_SCORE * 1.005:
            p_final = cand
            if sc < G_BEST_SCORE:
                update_best(cand, sc)
                p_final = normalize_prob(G_BEST_A)

    T_fin = 0.05 if p_final.size <= 2048 else 0.035
    T_end = 0.001 if p_final.size <= 2048 else 0.0007
    lr_fin = 0.10 if p_final.size <= 2048 else 0.08
    lr_end = 0.012

    if final_end > time.monotonic() + 2.0:
        optimize_from_p(p_final, final_end, rng, "final_polish",
                        T_fin, T_end, lr_fin, lr_end)

    # Prepare output.
    if G_BEST_A is None:
        out = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
    else:
        out = normalize_prob(G_BEST_A)
        if out.size > 8192:
            out = resample_mass(out, 4096)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(out.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            out = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        else:
            out = out / s

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out]
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        verified = internal_score(out_list)

    print("final internal=%.6f verified=%.6f len=%d time=%.1fs" %
          (G_BEST_SCORE, verified, len(out_list), time.monotonic() - start))
    return out_list
```
